# Topic candidates, wave-consensus corpus v2

Window: 2026-02-15 to 2026-08-27, post-cutoff for the 27B blind panel (verified
by cutoff-probe batch 4, 2026-08-27; 4B families probed later). V1 window
(2026-08-14..25) excluded: Aug extraction covers 08-01..08-13 and 08-26..27 only,
and v1 topics (corpus/topics.md, 30 kept) are not duplicated here.

Source: Wikipedia Portal:Current events, Feb-Aug 2026 (7 months, portals/*.txt),
extracted by tools/corpus_v2/extract_candidates.py -> 4,250 events, pre-filtered
by tools/corpus_v2/shortlist.py -> 1,270, curated month-by-month into
picks-2026-02..08.md. This file is the consolidated CANDIDATE pool: 251 primary
(plan was 250; July landed on 43 not 42 - open decision at spot-check: trim one
or keep) + 30 reserve. Selection is flagged per row.

Qualifying rules (CURATION_BRIEF.md): self-contained beat, 3-4+ independently
verifiable sub-facts (names, numbers, dates, places, orgs) to seed 40
propositions, military/ongoing-war <= 20% of each month's picks (actual: 17.5%
overall, 44/251), 1-2 beats per story max. Portal topic strings are kept
verbatim including portal mislabels (topic heading sometimes names an unrelated
story); flagged inline where relevant.

Fact-check pass COMPLETE (2026-08-27): all 281 rows done - 266 [V], 15 [V*]
(V2-003, V2-004, V2-013, V2-015, V2-018, V2-023, V2-030, V2-039, V2-062,
V2-078, V2-136, V2-143, V2-150, V2-188, R23), 0 [P]. Two [V] rows carry inline
DISPUTED-toll notes (V2-020 Minab school, V2-077 Kutum wedding) - kept as
proposition contradiction material. Convention from v1: [V] verified with
corrections inline, [V*] verified with open sub-facts listed. Per-topic
contamination gate probe runs AFTER the fact-check pass (NOTES.md Phase 5).

Domain spread of the 251 primary: politics 48, military 44, sports 34, law 37,
disaster 24, arts 17, economy 14, science 12, health 9, diplomacy 7, business 4,
tech 1. Reserve (30): sports 10, politics 8, law 4, military 4, disaster 2, arts
1, business 1.

Cross-month story tracking (decisions made at curation):
- Khamenei: 02-28 assassination day (Minab strike, V2-020), 03-01 Arafi council
  + named officials killed (V2-021, V2-022), 07-03 state funeral (V2-190).
- Yoon martial law: 02-19 life sentence (V2-009), 04-29 7-yr raise (reserve R10),
  06-12 treason 30-yr (V2-159), 06-22 Park Sung-jae 25-yr (V2-174), 07-27
  18-month suspended skipped.
- Iran war / Strait of Hormuz: one of the few deliberately multi-beat stories
  (ongoing war, ~20% cap); beats spread Feb-Jul, July ends the arc (V2-224..228).
- Unification Church: 06-22 dissolution dropped at curation (would be 3rd
  corpus beat), 07-16 Kweon kept (V2-203); 06-24 Lee Man-hee is Shincheonji
  (different church), kept (V2-177).
- Spain World Cup: two 07-19 beats (V2-210 final win, V2-211 records) - both
  kept, distinct fact sets (first to hold both men's and women's titles).

Status: CANDIDATES, not frozen. Freeze (prereg + tag) after fact-check pass.
